prt_cas_q,29,28,0	script	#EP_171_START	HIDDEN_WARP_NPC,2,2,{
	end;

OnTouch:
	if (terra_gloria_main == 25)
		cloakoffnpc "Nihil M. Heine#171_prtq",getcharid(0);
	end;
}

		mes "[Nihil]";
		mes "I came aqui porque I wanted to express meu gratitude.";
		mes "Obrigado to you, coisas have gotten better...";
		next;

		mes "Porem, nao tudo is indo as expected.";
		mes "Eu acho I may tem que pegar Peter's lugar ao inves.";
		next;
		select("What does esse significa?");
		cutin "ep16_nihi_miseria_heine01",2;
		mes "[Nihil]";
		mes "It aparece Eu sou proximo in line to o throne.";
		mes "Este is nao what I wanted.";
		next;
		cutin "ep16_nihi_miseria_heine04",2;
		mes "[Nihil]";
		mes "Todos else has um better certo to o throne, eles sao natural e qualificado fou it.";
		mes "Este is quite estranho e unfamiliar to me.";
		next;
		mes "[Nihil]";
		mes "Eu tenho been in alguns discussions com o Schwarz's government, entao Eu vou be ocupado fou um while...";
		next;
		cutin "ep16_nihi_miseria_heine01",2;
		mes "[Nihil]";
		mes "It parece to me they like este arrangement.";
		mes "I acreditar o relationship entre nosso two countries sera maintained in o future obrigado to nosso efforts.";
		next;
		mes "[Nihil]";
		mes "Se you aindum plum on atacando outro enterprise facilities, voce pode querer to rest primeiro.";
		mes "Fou o tempo being, you will precisar forca.";
		next;
		cutin "ep16_nihi_miseria_heine02",2;
		mes "[Nihil]";
		mes "I esperancum voce vai manter on indo.";
		mes "Obrigado novamente fou todos o ajudar.";
		mes "Eu tenho to ir agora.";
		terra_gloria_main = 26;
		close2;
		cutin "",255;
		cloakonnpc strnpcinfo(0),getcharid(0);
	}
	end;

	end;
}

einbech,219,80,3	script	Rebellion Crew#171_einb	4_M_REBELLION2,{
	if (terra_gloria_main == 26 && isbegin_quest(7850) == 0) {	// note: talking to this npc is not a prerequisite to the main quest
		mes "[Rebellion Crew]";
		mes "Hold on!";
		mes "Nos temos recentemente expanded o field of operations.";
		mes "Due to este expansion, nos temos been capaz de definir up um novo base in Einbroch.";
		next;
		mes "[Rebellion Crew]";
		mes "Deixe-me revel to you o novo bases location.";
		mes "nao longe de o Einbroch railway station... hum um pequeno bar chamado <NAVI>[Cat on Bullet]<INFO>einbroch,302,329,0,101,0</INFO></NAVI>, o proximo operation sera carried out la.";
		next;
		mes "[Rebellion Crew]";
		mes "Eu tenho delivered o informacao!";
		setquest 7850;
		close;
	}
	mes "[Rebellion Crew]";
	mes "E um pequeno bar proximo to o train station of Einbroch.";
	mes "<NAVI>[Cat on Bullet]<INFO>einbroch,302,329,0,101,0</INFO></NAVI>. Voce nao pensar e um cute name fou um pub?";
	close;

OnInit:
	cloakonnpc();
	questinfo(QTYPE_QUEST,QMARK_YELLOW,"terra_gloria_main == 26 && isbegin_quest(7850) == 0");
	end;
}

rebel_in,68,41,4	script	Rebellion Crew#171_rebelin		CLEAR_NPC,{
	if (isbegin_quest(11601) == 1) {
		mes "[Rebellion Crew]";
		mes "Oh! Bem-vindo back!";
		mes "Como estao you fazendo lately?";
		next;
		select("We encontrou um entry.");
		mes "[Rebellion Crew]";
		mes "I ver... There was um entrance...";
		mes "Desculpe fou o trouble.";
		mes "Eu sou heading back to Cat on Bullet e Eu vou be resting um bit.";
		next;
		select("Sobre Dien.");
		mes "[Rebellion Crew]";
		mes "Dien is having um cup of teum on o second floor, in a room.";
		completequest 11601;
		setquest 11602;
		close;
	}
	if (isbegin_quest(11602) == 1) {
		mes "[Rebellion Crew]";
		mes "Dien is having um cup of teum on o second floor, in a room.";
		close;
	}
	mes "[Rebellion Crew]";
	mes "What? Eu sou ocupado agorum mesmo!";
	close;

OnInit:
	setunitdata getnpcid(0),UNPC_CLASS,JOB_REBELLION;
	setunitdata getnpcid(0),UNPC_SEX,SEX_FEMALE;
	setunitdata getnpcid(0),UNPC_HAIRSTYLE,17;
	questinfo(QTYPE_QUEST,QMARK_YELLOW,"isbegin_quest(11601) == 1");
	end;
}

rebel_in,161,91,0	script	Ornament Box#171_rebelin	HIDDEN_NPC,{
	if (isbegin_quest(11602) == 1) {
		mes "- It contains um envelope. -";
		next;
		switch (select("Pegar um olhar.:Apenas pegar it.:Sair it.")) {
		case 1:
			cutin "ep171_trio_memory",1;
			mes "- E um velho picture com pessoas voce tem met antes. -";
			close3;

		case 2:
			mes "- I took o picture com me, vamos dar it back to Dien. -";
			completequest 11602;
			setquest 11603;
			getitem 25665,1;
			close;

		case 3:
			mes "- Cuidadosamente closed o box novamente. -";
			close;
		}
	}
	if (isbegin_quest(11603) == 1) {
		mes "- I took o picture com me, vamos dar it back to Dien. -";
		close;
	}
	mes "- E locked. -";
	close;

OnInit:
	questinfo(QTYPE_QUEST,QMARK_YELLOW,"isbegin_quest(11602) == 1");
	end;
}

				mes "O preparation fou o operation is nao yet completar ou seu party is performing outro operation.";
			break;
		case 3:
		}
		close;
	}
	end;

	cloakonnpc();
	end;
